####################################################################
#        TEST GENERATION BEGINS (DEEPMOSA + Devstral t=0.8)        #
####################################################################


# Prompt used at query #1
#--------------------------

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements
INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

Write unit test with pytest for function extract_text_array with the following signature: `def test_extract_text_array():`

import re

            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r

        for x in a

        result = result.strip()

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements
INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',

            output.append(None)

            if item:

            orp_buf[:] = []

# Prompt used at query #7

import re

            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators

        r.append(None)

        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:

        r.append(None)

    if strip_artifical_nl:

    return r

    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))

            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True

            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...

        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]

#--------------------------

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements
INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

Write unit test with pytest for function extract_text with the following signature: `def test_extract_text():`

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements

            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output

    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''

                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements
INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

Write unit test with pytest for function extract_text_array with the following signature: `def test_extract_text_array():`

    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:

    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))

# Prompt used at query #34

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements
INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

    return output

    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''

# Prompt used at query #47

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements
INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',

    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output

    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators

        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements
INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

Write unit test with pytest for function extract_text with the following signature: `def test_extract_text():`

            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:

    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements
INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

Write unit test with pytest for function extract_text_array with the following signature: `def test_extract_text_array():`

    return output

        return ''

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements
INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',

    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

        return ''

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements
INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',

    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []

import re


# https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Inline_elements#Elements
INLINE_TAGS = {
    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

    'a', 'abbr', 'acronym', 'b', 'bdo', 'big', 'br', 'button', 'cite',
    'code', 'dfn', 'em', 'i', 'img', 'input', 'kbd', 'label', 'map',
    'object', 'q', 'samp', 'script', 'select', 'small', 'span', 'strong',
    'sub', 'sup', 'textarea', 'time', 'tt', 'var'
}

SEPARATORS = {'br'}


# Definition of whitespace in HTML:
# https://www.w3.org/TR/html4/struct/text.html#h-9.1
WHITESPACE_RE = re.compile('[\x20\x09\x0C\u200B\x0A\x0D]+')


def squash_html_whitespace(text):
    # use raw extract_text for preformatted content (like <pre> content or set
    # by CSS rules)
    # apply this function on top of
    return WHITESPACE_RE.sub(' ', text)


def _squash_artifical_nl(parts):
    output, last_nl = [], False
    for x in parts:
        if x is not None:
            output.append(x)
            last_nl = False
        elif not last_nl:
            output.append(None)
            last_nl = True
    return output


def _strip_artifical_nl(parts):
    if not parts:
        return parts
    for start_idx, pt in enumerate(parts):
        if isinstance(pt, str):
            # 0, 1, 2, index of first string [start_idx:...
            break
    iterator = enumerate(parts[:start_idx - 1 if start_idx > 0 else None:-1])
    for end_idx, pt in iterator:
        if isinstance(pt, str):  # 0=None, 1=-1, 2=-2, index of last string
            break
    return parts[start_idx:-end_idx if end_idx > 0 else None]


def _merge_original_parts(parts):
    output, orp_buf = [], []

    def flush():
        if orp_buf:
            item = squash_html_whitespace(''.join(orp_buf)).strip()
            if item:
                output.append(item)
            orp_buf[:] = []

    for x in parts:
        if not isinstance(x, str):
            flush()
            output.append(x)
        else:
            orp_buf.append(x)
    flush()
    return output


def extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=True, strip_artifical_nl=True):
    if callable(dom.tag):
        return ''
    r = []
    if dom.tag in SEPARATORS:
        r.append(True)  # equivalent of '\n' used to designate separators
    elif dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if dom.text is not None:
        r.append(dom.text)
    for child in dom.getchildren():
        r.extend(extract_text_array(child, squash_artifical_nl=False,
                                    strip_artifical_nl=False))
        if child.tail is not None:
            r.append(child.tail)
    if dom.tag not in INLINE_TAGS and dom.tag not in SEPARATORS:
        # equivalent of '\n' used to designate artificially inserted newlines
        r.append(None)
    if squash_artifical_nl:
        r = _squash_artifical_nl(r)
    if strip_artifical_nl:
        r = _strip_artifical_nl(r)
    return r


def extract_text(dom, block_symbol='\n', sep_symbol='\n', squash_space=True):
    a = extract_text_array(dom, squash_artifical_nl=squash_space)
    if squash_space:
        a = _strip_artifical_nl(_squash_artifical_nl(_merge_original_parts(a)))
    result = ''.join(
        block_symbol if x is None else (
            sep_symbol if x is True else x
        )
        for x in a
    )
    if squash_space:
        result = result.strip()
    return result

Write unit test with pytest for function extract_text with the following signature: `def test_extract_text():`
